# ArkUI API Knowledge Base（按范式）

> 更新时间：2026-02-06
> 适用仓库：`../../../interface/sdk-js/api`
> 文档目标：按“类web / lite / ArkTS Dynamic / ArkTS Static”四种口径，整理 API 目录、文档入口、导入方式和示例

## 0. 前置知识与口径

- ArkTS Dynamic 的ArkUI组件：`../../../interface/sdk-js/api/arkui/component`，文件名不含 `static.d.ets`
- ArkTS Static 的ArkUI组件：`../../../interface/sdk-js/api/arkui/component`，文件名含 `static.d.ets`
- arkui 公共：`../../../interface/sdk-js/api/arkui`
- FutureAbility（类web）full：`../../../interface/sdk-js/api/common/full/featureability.d.ts`
- FutureAbility（类web）lite：`../../../interface/sdk-js/api/common/lite/featureability.d.ts`

当前仓快照结论（2026-02-06）：

- `arkui/component` 中非 `static.d.ets` 文件数为 `0`
- `arkui/component` 中 `static.d.ets` 文件数为 `129`
- 因此“ArkTS Dynamic组件目录”在本分支下是空集，ArkTS Dynamic能力主要体现在 `@ohos.*.d.ts` 模块 API

## 1. 类web

### 涉及API目录

- `full` 声明入口：`../../../interface/sdk-js/api/common/full/featureability.d.ts`
- 组件共用目录：`../../../interface/sdk-js/api/arkui/component`
- 模块化替代入口（注解 `@useinstead` 指向）：`ohos.ability.featureAbility.FeatureAbility`

建议把“类web”理解为历史兼容层：

- 核心类是 `FeatureAbility`
- 关键能力是“启动 Ability / 调 Ability / 订阅事件 / 设备消息”
- 文件内存在大量 `@deprecated since 8`

### 文档

`featureability.d.ts`（full）中的主要类型与职责：

- `Result`
- `SubscribeMessageResponse`
- `CallAbilityParam`
- `SubscribeAbilityEventParam`
- `SendMessageOptions`
- `SubscribeMessageOptions`
- `RequestParams`
- `FinishWithResultParams`
- `FeatureAbility`

`FeatureAbility`（full）方法清单：

- `startAbility(request: RequestParams): Promise<Result>`
- `startAbilityForResult(request: RequestParams): Promise<Result>`
- `finishWithResult(param: FinishWithResultParams): Promise<Result>`
- `getDeviceList(flag: number): Promise<Result>`
- `callAbility(param: CallAbilityParam): Promise<string>`
- `continueAbility(): Promise<Result>`
- `subscribeAbilityEvent(param: SubscribeAbilityEventParam, func: Function): Promise<string>`
- `unsubscribeAbilityEvent(param: SubscribeAbilityEventParam): Promise<string>`
- `sendMsg(options: SendMessageOptions): void`
- `subscribeMsg(options: SubscribeMessageOptions): void`
- `unsubscribeMsg(): void`

`RequestParams` 常用字段：

- `bundleName?: string`
- `abilityName?: string`
- `entities?: Array<string>`
- `action?: string`
- `deviceType?: number`
- `data?: object`
- `flag?: number`
- `url?: string`

`CallAbilityParam` 常用字段：

- `bundleName: string`
- `abilityName: string`
- `messageCode: number`
- `abilityType: number`
- `data?: object`
- `syncOption?: number`

解读要点：

- full 文件虽然名字是 `full`，但注解中大量能力标注为 `SystemCapability.ArkUI.ArkUI.Lite`
- full/lite 的差异主要体现在“是否包含 start/call/event 这组能力”，不是 `syscap` 字面不同
- 文档层面应明确其“历史兼容”属性，避免在新代码中继续扩大依赖

### 全局导入的API

类web范式中，常见使用方式是直接使用全局对象 `FeatureAbility`：

- `FeatureAbility.startAbility`
- `FeatureAbility.startAbilityForResult`
- `FeatureAbility.finishWithResult`
- `FeatureAbility.getDeviceList`
- `FeatureAbility.callAbility`
- `FeatureAbility.continueAbility`
- `FeatureAbility.subscribeAbilityEvent`
- `FeatureAbility.unsubscribeAbilityEvent`
- `FeatureAbility.sendMsg`
- `FeatureAbility.subscribeMsg`
- `FeatureAbility.unsubscribeMsg`

建议：

- 若业务在迁移期，保留 `FeatureAbility` 调用面
- 新增能力优先使用替代模块 API（`@useinstead` 指向）

### 举例

示例1：启动目标 Ability。

```ts
FeatureAbility.startAbility({
  bundleName: 'com.example.demo',
  abilityName: 'EntryAbility',
  url: 'pages/Index'
}).then((ret) => {
  console.info(`startAbility code=${ret.code}`)
})
```

示例2：带返回结果启动。

```ts
FeatureAbility.startAbilityForResult({
  bundleName: 'com.example.demo',
  abilityName: 'DetailAbility',
  data: { from: 'home' }
}).then((ret) => {
  console.info(`result code=${ret.code}`)
  console.info(JSON.stringify(ret.data))
})
```

示例3：调用 AA 能力。

```ts
FeatureAbility.callAbility({
  bundleName: 'com.example.service',
  abilityName: 'RemoteServiceAbility',
  messageCode: 1001,
  abilityType: 0,
  data: { op: 'query' }
}).then((json) => {
  console.info(`callAbility result=${json}`)
})
```

示例4：订阅与取消订阅 Ability 事件。

```ts
const eventParam = {
  bundleName: 'com.example.service',
  abilityName: 'RemoteServiceAbility',
  messageCode: 2001,
  abilityType: 0
}

FeatureAbility.subscribeAbilityEvent(eventParam, (event: object) => {
  console.info(`event=${JSON.stringify(event)}`)
})

FeatureAbility.unsubscribeAbilityEvent(eventParam)
```

示例5：跨设备消息收发。

```ts
FeatureAbility.subscribeMsg({
  success: (msg) => {
    console.info(`recv ${msg.message} from ${msg.deviceId}`)
  },
  fail: (data, code) => {
    console.error(`subscribeMsg fail code=${code} data=${data}`)
  }
})

FeatureAbility.sendMsg({
  deviceId: 'device-001',
  bundleName: 'com.example.demo',
  abilityName: 'EntryAbility',
  message: 'ping'
})
```

## 2. 轻量是full的子集

### 涉及API目录

- `lite` 声明入口：`../../../interface/sdk-js/api/common/lite/featureability.d.ts`
- `full` 对照入口：`../../../interface/sdk-js/api/common/full/featureability.d.ts`
- 组件共用目录：`../../../interface/sdk-js/api/arkui/component`

子集关系（按当前声明）：

- lite 仅保留消息相关接口
- full 包含 lite 的消息接口，并额外包含 start/call/event/continue 能力

### 文档

`lite/featureability.d.ts` 类型构成：

- `SubscribeMessageResponse`
- `SendMessageOptions`
- `SubscribeMessageOptions`
- `FeatureAbility`

`FeatureAbility`（lite）方法清单：

- `sendMsg(options: SendMessageOptions): void`
- `subscribeMsg(options: SubscribeMessageOptions): void`
- `unsubscribeMsg(): void`

关键字段口径：

- `SendMessageOptions.deviceId`
- `SendMessageOptions.bundleName`
- `SendMessageOptions.abilityName`
- `SendMessageOptions.message?`
- `SendMessageOptions.success?`
- `SendMessageOptions.fail?`
- `SendMessageOptions.complete?`
- `SubscribeMessageOptions.success?`
- `SubscribeMessageOptions.fail?`

共同注解特征：

- `@syscap SystemCapability.ArkUI.ArkUI.Lite`
- `@since 5 dynamiconly`
- `@deprecated since 8`

和 full 的差异矩阵：

- `startAbility`：full 有，lite 无
- `startAbilityForResult`：full 有，lite 无
- `finishWithResult`：full 有，lite 无
- `getDeviceList`：full 有，lite 无
- `callAbility`：full 有，lite 无
- `continueAbility`：full 有，lite 无
- `subscribeAbilityEvent`：full 有，lite 无
- `unsubscribeAbilityEvent`：full 有，lite 无
- `sendMsg`：full 有，lite 有
- `subscribeMsg`：full 有，lite 有
- `unsubscribeMsg`：full 有，lite 有

### 全局导入的API

轻量子集可用的全局 API：

- `FeatureAbility.sendMsg`
- `FeatureAbility.subscribeMsg`
- `FeatureAbility.unsubscribeMsg`

工程建议：

- 若仅需“设备消息收发”，优先使用 lite 子集口径描述
- 若需要“启动 Ability / 结果回传 / 订阅 AA 事件”，必须转到 full 口径
- 文档里要明确“lite 是 full 子集”，避免功能预期超出

### 举例

示例1：注册消息监听。

```ts
FeatureAbility.subscribeMsg({
  success: (resp) => {
    console.info(`deviceId=${resp.deviceId}`)
    console.info(`bundleName=${resp.bundleName}`)
    console.info(`abilityName=${resp.abilityName}`)
    console.info(`message=${resp.message}`)
  },
  fail: (data, code) => {
    console.error(`subscribe fail code=${code}, data=${data}`)
  }
})
```

示例2：发送轻量消息。

```ts
FeatureAbility.sendMsg({
  deviceId: 'remote-device-id',
  bundleName: 'com.example.target',
  abilityName: 'EntryAbility',
  message: 'hello-lite',
  success: () => {
    console.info('sendMsg success')
  },
  complete: () => {
    console.info('sendMsg complete')
  }
})
```

示例3：退出时取消监听。

```ts
aboutToDisappear() {
  FeatureAbility.unsubscribeMsg()
}
```

## 3. ArkTS Dynamic

### 涉及API目录

- ArkTS Dynamic组件 TypeScript 目录：`../../../interface/sdk-js/api/arkui/component`
- ArkUI 公共目录：`../../../interface/sdk-js/api/arkui`
- 模块 API 目录：`../../../interface/sdk-js/api/@ohos.*.d.ts`

推荐关注的ArkTS Dynamic模块文档：

- 路由：`../../../interface/sdk-js/api/@ohos.router.d.ts`
- 弹窗提示：`../../../interface/sdk-js/api/@ohos.promptAction.d.ts`
- Ability：`../../../interface/sdk-js/api/@ohos.ability.featureAbility.d.ts`
- 上下文：`../../../interface/sdk-js/api/@ohos.app.ability.*.d.ts`

当前快照说明：

- `arkui/component` 下非 `static.d.ets` 文件数为 `0`
- 因此“ArkTS Dynamic组件声明文件”在当前分支为空
- 但ArkTS Dynamic模块 API（`@ohos.*`）是完整存在的

### 文档

ArkTS Dynamic需要区分两类调用面：

- 组件构建：`Text/Button/Column/...` 这类 DSL 组件
- 系统 API：通过 `import xxx from '@ohos.xxx'` 模块导入

`@ohos.router.d.ts` 可见结构：

- `declare namespace router`
- 末尾 `export default router`
- 常见方法包括 `pushUrl`、`replaceUrl`、`back`

`@ohos.promptAction.d.ts` 可见结构：

- `declare namespace promptAction`
- 含 `ShowToastOptions` 等配置类型
- ArkTS Dynamic场景里常用于轻提示

ArkTS Dynamic文档编写要点：

- 组件调用写法按“全局组件函数”描述
- 模块能力按“模块默认导出对象”描述
- 对同名能力要区分“组件 API”和“系统模块 API”

### 组件是全局导入的API API是按模块导入的

组件（全局使用，不写 import）示例：

- `Text`
- `Button`
- `Column`
- `Row`
- `List`
- `Image`
- `Web`
- `Navigation`

系统 API（按模块 import）示例：

- `router` from `@ohos.router`
- `promptAction` from `@ohos.promptAction`
- `mediaquery` from `@ohos.mediaquery`
- `hilog` from `@ohos.hilog`
- `intl` from `@ohos.intl`

导入规则总结：

- 组件：默认全局可用
- API：必须按模块名导入
- 模块签名以 `@ohos.*.d.ts` 为准

常见误区：

- 误区1：把 `router` 当全局对象直接使用
- 误区2：给 `Text`、`Button` 写模块导入
- 误区3：把ArkTS Static声明文件路径当成ArkTS Dynamic模块导入路径

### 举例

示例1：组件 + 路由 + toast。

```ts
import router from '@ohos.router'
import promptAction from '@ohos.promptAction'

@Entry
@Component
struct Index {
  build() {
    Column() {
      Text('Dynamic JS Pattern')
      Button('Go Detail').onClick(() => {
        router.pushUrl({ url: 'pages/Detail' })
        promptAction.showToast({ message: 'navigating...' })
      })
    }
  }
}
```

示例2：`pushUrl` Promise 风格。

```ts
import router from '@ohos.router'

router.pushUrl({ url: 'pages/Profile' })
  .then(() => {
    console.info('pushUrl success')
  })
  .catch((err: Error) => {
    console.error(`pushUrl failed: ${JSON.stringify(err)}`)
  })
```

示例3：`replaceUrl` 与 `back`。

```ts
import router from '@ohos.router'

function goHome() {
  router.replaceUrl({ url: 'pages/Home' })
}

function goBack() {
  router.back()
}
```

示例4：仅组件 DSL（不导入组件）。

```ts
@Entry
@Component
struct PlainComponentPage {
  build() {
    Column() {
      Text('Components are global')
      Button('OK')
      Row() {
        Text('Left')
        Text('Right')
      }
    }
  }
}
```

示例5：模块 API 统一收口写法。

```ts
import router from '@ohos.router'
import promptAction from '@ohos.promptAction'

export function openPage(url: string) {
  router.pushUrl({ url }).then(() => {
    promptAction.showToast({ message: `opened ${url}` })
  })
}
```

## 4. ArkTS Static

### 涉及API目录

- ArkTS Static组件 TypeScript：`../../../interface/sdk-js/api/arkui/component`（文件名含 `static.d.ets`）
- ArkTS Static的ArkUI 公共声明：`../../../interface/sdk-js/api/arkui/*.static.d.ets`
- 代表文件：
- `../../../interface/sdk-js/api/arkui/component/button.static.d.ets`
- `../../../interface/sdk-js/api/arkui/component/common.static.d.ets`
- `../../../interface/sdk-js/api/arkui/component/enums.static.d.ets`
- `../../../interface/sdk-js/api/arkui/component/units.static.d.ets`

ArkTS Static目录快照（2026-02-06）：

- `*.static.d.ets` 数量：`129`
- 文件头常见标记：`"use static"` 或 `'use static'`

### 文档

ArkTS Static声明的主结构：

- 组件函数：`export declare function ComponentName(...)`
- Attribute：`ComponentNameAttribute extends CommonMethod`
- Options：`ComponentNameOptions`
- 事件/回调：`OnXxx`、`XxxEvent`、`XxxCallback`
- 枚举：`XxxMode`、`XxxType`、`XxxAlign`

`button.static.d.ets` 可见的关键声明：

- `ButtonConfiguration`
- `ButtonOptions`
- `ButtonLabelStyle`
- `ButtonAttribute`
- `Button(...)`（重载）

`common.static.d.ets` 提供全局基座：

- `CommonMethod`
- `AttributeModifier<T>`
- `ContentModifier<T>`
- `CommonConfiguration<T>`
- `UICommonEvent`
- `UIGestureEvent`
- `UIScrollableCommonEvent`
- `ScrollableCommonMethod`

`common.static.d.ets` 可见全局函数：

- `postCardAction(component: Object, action: Object): void`
- `$r(value: string, ...params): Resource`
- `$rawfile(value: string): Resource`
- `animateToImmediately(value: AnimateParam, processor: VoidCallback): void`
- `makeBindable<T>(value: T, onChange: Callback<T>): Bindable<T>`
- `$$<T>(value: T): Bindable<T>`
- `applyStyles<T extends CommonMethod>(this: T, customStyles: CustomStyles): T`

ArkTS Static组件样例分组（按文件名）：

- 布局：`row`、`column`、`stack`、`flex`、`relativeContainer`、`grid`
- 列表滚动：`list`、`listItem`、`listItemGroup`、`scroll`、`waterFlow`
- 文本输入：`text`、`span`、`textInput`、`textArea`、`search`
- 选择器：`datePicker`、`timePicker`、`textPicker`、`calendarPicker`
- 媒体：`image`、`video`、`canvas`、`web`
- 导航：`navigation`、`navDestination`、`tabs`、`tabContent`
- 图形：`line`、`rect`、`circle`、`ellipse`、`path`、`shape`

ArkTS Static范式写文档时建议表达：

- 组件是全局可用入口
- 类型定义以 `Component + Attribute + Options` 三段式为主
- 通用能力优先引用 `common/enums/units`
- 文件中 `@syscap` 和 `@since` 是能力边界依据

### 全局导入的API

ArkTS Static范式常见全局组件 API：

- `Text`
- `Button`
- `Column`
- `Row`
- `Stack`
- `List`
- `ListItem`
- `Grid`
- `Image`
- `Video`
- `Canvas`
- `Web`
- `Navigation`
- `NavDestination`
- `Tabs`
- `TabContent`
- `TextInput`
- `TextArea`
- `Search`
- `DatePicker`
- `TimePicker`
- `TextPicker`
- `Slider`
- `Toggle`
- `Progress`
- `LoadingProgress`

ArkTS Static范式常见全局函数 API：

- `$r`
- `$rawfile`
- `postCardAction`
- `animateToImmediately`
- `makeBindable`
- `$$`
- `applyStyles`

--- .claude/skills/compile-analysis/scripts/analyze_compile.sh ---
1: #!/bin/bash
2: #
3: # 编译效率分析脚本
4: # 功能：分析单个文件的编译时间、资源开销和头文件依赖
5: #
6: # ⚠️ 重要要求：
7: #   1. 编译命令必须在 out/{product} 目录下执行
8: #   2. 分析结果必须基于实际运行结果，不能推测
9: #   3. 头文件依赖必须通过 parse_ii.py 解析 .ii 文件
10: #
11: # 使用方法:
12: #   ./analyze_compile.sh <源文件路径> [产品名称] [选项]
13: #
14: # 示例:
15: #   ./analyze_compile.sh frameworks/core/event/touch_event.cpp
16: #   ./analyze_compile.sh frameworks/core/event/touch_event.cpp rk3568
17: #   ./analyze_compile.sh frameworks/core/event/touch_event.cpp rk3568 --save-script
18: #
19: # 选项:
20: #   --save-script    保存增强编译脚本到 out/{product}/compile_single_file_{name}.sh
21: #                    可用于后续重复执行独立的性能测试
22: 
23: set -e
24: 
25: # 获取脚本所在目录
26: SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
27: 
28: # 查找 OpenHarmony 根目录
29: find_oh_root() {
30:     local dir="$(pwd)"
31:     while [[ ! -f "$dir/.gn" ]]; do
32:         dir="$(dirname "$dir")"
33:         if [[ "$dir" == "/" ]]; then
34:             echo "错误: 找不到 OpenHarmony 根目录（无 .gn 文件）" >&2
35:             return 1
36:         fi
37:     done
38:     echo "$dir"
39: }
40: 

--- .claude/skills/openharmony-build/scripts/analyze_build_error.sh ---
1: #!/bin/bash
2: # OpenHarmony Build Error Analysis Script
3: # Analyzes build logs and extracts relevant error information
4: 
5: set -e
6: 
7: # Color codes for output
8: RED='\033[0;31m'
9: YELLOW='\033[1;33m'
10: GREEN='\033[0;32m'
11: NC='\033[0m' # No Color
12: 
13: # OpenHarmony root directory
14: # Script is at: .../ace_engine/.claude/skills/openharmony-build/scripts/
15: # Need to go up 7 levels to reach OpenHarmony root
16: SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
17: OH_ROOT="$(cd "$SCRIPT_DIR/../../../../../../.." && pwd)"
18: 
19: echo -e "${GREEN}OpenHarmony Build Error Analyzer${NC}"
20: echo "OpenHarmony Root: $OH_ROOT"
21: echo ""
22: 
23: # Check if product name is provided
24: if [ -z "$1" ]; then
25:     echo -e "${YELLOW}Usage: $0 <product-name>${NC}"
26:     echo "Example: $0 rk3568"
27:     echo ""
28:     echo "Available products in out/:"
29:     ls -1 "$OH_ROOT/out/" 2>/dev/null | grep -v "sdk\|gen\|kernel" || echo "  No products found"
30:     exit 1
31: fi
32: 
33: PRODUCT_NAME="$1"
34: BUILD_LOG="$OH_ROOT/out/$PRODUCT_NAME/build.log"
35: 
36: # Check if build log exists
37: if [ ! -f "$BUILD_LOG" ]; then
38:     echo -e "${RED}Error: Build log not found: $BUILD_LOG${NC}"
39:     echo ""
40:     echo "Searching for alternative log files..."

--- .claude/skills/openharmony-build/scripts/check_fast_rebuild.sh ---
1: #!/bin/bash
2: # Helper script to determine if fast-rebuild can be used
3: # Checks if any GN files (BUILD.gn, *.gni) have been modified recently
4: 
5: set -e
6: 
7: # Color codes
8: GREEN='\033[0;32m'
9: YELLOW='\033[1;33m'
10: RED='\033[0;31m'
11: NC='\033[0m' # No Color
12: 
13: # Find OpenHarmony root
14: SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
15: OH_ROOT="$(cd "$SCRIPT_DIR/../../../../../../.." && pwd)"
16: 
17: # Default time window in minutes (check for changes in last N minutes)
18: TIME_WINDOW=${1:-30}
19: 
20: echo -e "${GREEN}OpenHarmony Fast Rebuild Checker${NC}"
21: echo "OpenHarmony Root: $OH_ROOT"
22: echo "Time window: last $TIME_WINDOW minutes"
23: echo ""
24: 
25: # Check if build output exists
26: if [ ! -d "$OH_ROOT/out" ]; then
27:     echo -e "${RED}✗ No build output found${NC}"
28:     echo "This appears to be a first-time build."
29:     echo "Recommended: Use standard build command"
30:     echo "  ./build.sh --export-para PYCACHE_ENABLE:true --product-name rk3568 --ccache"
31:     exit 0
32: fi
33: 
34: # Find recently modified GN files
35: echo -e "${YELLOW}Checking for recently modified GN files...${NC}"
36: 
37: RECENT_GN_FILES=$(find "$OH_ROOT" -type f \( -name "BUILD.gn" -o -name "*.gni" \) -mmin -$TIME_WINDOW 2>/dev/null || true)
38: 
39: if [ -z "$RECENT_GN_FILES" ]; then
40:     echo -e "${GREEN}✓ No GN files modified in the last $TIME_WINDOW minutes${NC}"

--- .claude/skills/openharmony-build/scripts/find_recent_errors.sh ---
1: #!/bin/bash
2: # Find recent build errors in OpenHarmony build logs
3: # Useful for quick error checking during active development
4: 
5: set -e
6: 
7: # OpenHarmony root directory
8: # Script is at: .../ace_engine/.claude/skills/openharmony-build/scripts/
9: # Need to go up 7 levels to reach OpenHarmony root
10: SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
11: OH_ROOT="$(cd "$SCRIPT_DIR/../../../../../../.." && pwd)"
12: 
13: # Default product if none specified
14: PRODUCT="${1:-rk3568}"
15: BUILD_LOG="$OH_ROOT/out/$PRODUCT/build.log"
16: 
17: if [ ! -f "$BUILD_LOG" ]; then
18:     echo "Build log not found: $BUILD_LOG"
19:     echo ""
20:     echo "Usage: $0 [product-name]"
21:     echo "Example: $0 rk3568"
22:     exit 1
23: fi
24: 
25: echo "Searching for recent errors in: $BUILD_LOG"
26: echo ""
27: 
28: # Show last 20 lines with "error:"
29: echo "=== Recent error lines (last 20) ==="
30: grep -i "error:" "$BUILD_LOG" 2>/dev/null | tail -20
31: echo ""
32: 
33: # Show last 10 fatal errors
34: echo "=== Recent fatal errors (last 10) ==="
35: grep -i "fatal" "$BUILD_LOG" 2>/dev/null | tail -10
36: echo ""
37: 
38: # Show FAILED sections
39: echo "=== FAILED sections (last 5) ==="
40: grep -B 2 "FAILED:" "$BUILD_LOG" 2>/dev/null | tail -20

1: #!/bin/bash
2: # Copyright (c) 2025 Huawei Device Co., Ltd.
3: # Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the "License");
4: # you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.

13: # limitations under the License.
14: 
15: set -euo pipefail
16: 
17: execute_script() {
18:     local working_dir="$1"
19:     local script_path="$2"
20:     shift 2
21:     local original_dir=$(pwd)
22:     cd "$working_dir" || {
23:         echo "错误: 无法进入工作目录 $working_dir"
24:         return 1
25:     }
26:     "$script_path" "$@"
27:     local result=$?
28:     cd "$original_dir"
29: 
30:     return $result
31: }
32: 
33: # arkoala-arkts preprocess
34: execute_script "./arkoala-arkts" "../tools/annotate.py" "." "annotate-config.json"
35: execute_script "./arkoala-arkts" "../../arkoala/process_arkoala.py" "."
36: # runtime preprocess
37: execute_script "./incremental/runtime" "../../tools/annotate.py" "." "annotate-config.json"
38: # regenerate all gni files
39: execute_script "./arkoala-arkts" "../tools/gen_gni.py" "--json" "arktsconfig-gn.json" "--output" "components.gni" "--gn-tool" "../../../../../../../../prebuilts/build-tools/linux-x86/bin/gn"
40: execute_script "./incremental/runtime" "../../tools/gen_gni.py" "--json" "arktsconfig-gn.json" "--output" "runtime.gni" "--gn-tool" "../../../../../../../../../prebuilts/build-tools/linux-x86/bin/gn"

--- .claude/skills/capi-test-fixer/scripts/diagnose.py ---
1: #!/usr/bin/env python3
2: """
3: C API Test Fixer - Main diagnosis script
4: Automatically diagnose and fix common C API unit test failures in OpenHarmony ACE Engine.
5: """
6: 
7: import os
8: import sys
9: import re
10: import argparse
11: import json
12: from pathlib import Path
13: from typing import List, Dict, Optional, Tuple
14: 
15: # Add parent directory to path for imports
16: sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))
17: 
18: # Load pattern modules
19: def load_patterns():
20:     """Load patterns from JSON file or use defaults."""
21:     patterns_json_path = os.path.join(
22:         os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))),
23:         "patterns",
24:         "error_patterns.json"
25:     )
26: 
27:     if os.path.exists(patterns_json_path):
28:         try:
29:             with open(patterns_json_path, 'r') as f:
30:                 return json.load(f)
31:         except (json.JSONDecodeError, IOError) as e:
32:             print(f"Warning: Could not load patterns from {patterns_json_path}: {e}")
33: 
34:     # Define default patterns if not available
35:     return {
36:         "static_modifier_missing": {
37:             "regex": r"undefined symbol.*Get(\w+)StaticModifier",
38:             "category": "static_modifier",
39:             "severity": "high",
40:             "fix_template": "static_modifier"

16: #ifndef FOUNDATION_ACE_FRAMEWORKS_BASE_UTILS_CONTAINER_SCOPE_CONTAINER_SCOPE_DIAGNOSTICS_H
17: #define FOUNDATION_ACE_FRAMEWORKS_BASE_UTILS_CONTAINER_SCOPE_CONTAINER_SCOPE_DIAGNOSTICS_H
18: 
19: #include <cstdint>
20: #include <string>
21: 
22: namespace OHOS::Ace {
23: 
24: // Bitmask flags describing anomalies detected during PushCurrent/PopCurrent operations.
25: // Multiple anomalies can be combined via bitwise OR.
26: enum class FrameAnomaly : uint32_t {
27:     NONE = 0,
28:     UNPAIRED_PUSH = 1 << 0,    // Push without a corresponding Pop (e.g., Container::PushCurrent init)
29:     STACK_UNDERFLOW = 1 << 1,  // Pop called with uid not found in active stack
30:     VALUE_MISMATCH = 1 << 2,   // Pop restoreId differs from the oldValue saved at Push time
31:     OUT_OF_ORDER_POP = 1 << 3, // Pop uid is not the top of the active stack (nested scope violated)
32: };
33: 
34: // Bitwise operators for combining and testing FrameAnomaly flags.
35: inline FrameAnomaly operator|(FrameAnomaly a, FrameAnomaly b)
36: {
37:     return static_cast<FrameAnomaly>(static_cast<uint32_t>(a) | static_cast<uint32_t>(b));
38: }
39: 
40: inline uint32_t operator&(FrameAnomaly a, FrameAnomaly b)

30: {

36:     auto marginOffset = contentOffset_ - offset;
37:     canvas.AttachPen(pen);
38:     RSRect paintRect = RSRect(-marginOffset.GetX() - paddingOffset_.GetX(),
39:         -marginOffset.GetY() - paddingOffset_.GetY(),
40:         frameMarginSize_.Width() - HALF_STROKE_WIDTH_OFFSET - marginOffset.GetX() - paddingOffset_.GetX(),

--- .claude/skills/build-error-analyzer/script/extract_last_error.sh (truncated) ---
1: #!/bin/bash
2: #
3: # Script to extract the last complete error from OpenHarmony build logs
4: # Usage: ./extract_last_error.sh [build_log_path]
5: #
6: 
7: set -e
8: 
9: # Configuration
10: BUILD_DIR="${PWD}/out"
11: OUTPUT_FILE="last_error.log"
12: PRODUCT_NAME="${PRODUCT_NAME:-rk3568}"
13: 
14: # Output file will be placed in the same directory as build log
15: # Extract log directory for later use
16: LOG_DIR=""
17: 
18: # Allow custom build log path
19: if [ -n "$1" ]; then
20:     BUILD_LOG="$1"
21: else
22:     # Try to find build.log in common locations
23:     POSSIBLE_LOGS=(
24:         "out/${PRODUCT_NAME}/build.log"
25:         "out/build.log"
26:         "${BUILD_DIR}/${PRODUCT_NAME}/build.log"
27:         "${BUILD_DIR}/build.log"
28:     )
29: 
30:     BUILD_LOG=""
31:     for log in "${POSSIBLE_LOGS[@]}"; do
32:         if [ -f "$log" ]; then
33:             BUILD_LOG="$log"
34:             break
35:         fi
36:     done
37: fi
38: 
39: # If still no log found, check if it's in the current directory
40: if [ -z "$BUILD_LOG" ] && [ -f "build.log" ]; then
41:     BUILD_LOG="build.log"
42: fi
43: 
44: # Check if build log exists
45: if [ -z "$BUILD_LOG" ]; then
46:     echo "Error: Build log not found!" >&2
47:     echo "Searched locations:" >&2
48:     for log in "${POSSIBLE_LOGS[@]}"; do
49:         echo "  - ${log}" >&2
50:     done
51:     echo "You can specify the log path as: $0 /path/to/build.log" >&2
52:     exit 1
53: fi
54: 
55: # Extract directory path for output file
56: LOG_DIR=$(dirname "$BUILD_LOG")
57: 
58: # Set output file to same directory as build log
59: OUTPUT_FILE="${LOG_DIR}/last_error.log"
60: 
61: echo "Using build log: $BUILD_LOG"
62: echo "Output file: $OUTPUT_FILE"
63: 
64: # Function to extract the last complete error
65: extract_last_error() {
66:     local log_file="$1"
67:     local output="$2"
68: 
69:     # Use Python to reliably extract the error block
70:     # This approach: find the last [N/M] CXX block that contains errors
71:     python3 - "$log_file" "$output" << 'PYTHON_SCRIPT'
72: import sys
73: import re
74: 
75: def extract_last_error(log_file, output_file):
76:     try:
77:         with open(log_file, 'r', encoding='utf-8', errors='ignore') as f:
78:             lines = f.readlines()
79:     except Exception as e:
80:         with open(output_file, 'w') as f:

1: #!/usr/bin/env python3
2: """
3: C API Test Fixer - Main diagnosis script
4: Automatically diagnose and fix common C API unit test failures in OpenHarmony ACE Engine.
5: """
6: 
7: import os
8: import sys
9: import re
10: import argparse
11: import json
12: from pathlib import Path
13: from typing import List, Dict, Optional, Tuple
14: 
15: # Add parent directory to path for imports
16: sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))
17: 
18: # Load pattern modules
19: def load_patterns():
20:     """Load patterns from JSON file or use defaults."""
21:     patterns_json_path = os.path.join(
22:         os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))),
23:         "patterns",
24:         "error_patterns.json"
25:     )
26: 
27:     if os.path.exists(patterns_json_path):
28:         try:
29:             with open(patterns_json_path, 'r') as f:
30:                 return json.load(f)
31:         except (json.JSONDecodeError, IOError) as e:
32:             print(f"Warning: Could not load patterns from {patterns_json_path}: {e}")
33: 
34:     # Define default patterns if not available
35:     return {
36:         "static_modifier_missing": {
37:             "regex": r"undefined symbol.*Get(\w+)StaticModifier",
38:             "category": "static_modifier",
39:             "severity": "high",
40:             "fix_template": "static_modifier"
41:         },
42:         "converter_api_misuse": {
43:             "regex": r"GetOptPtr\(\&",
44:             "category": "converter_api",
45:             "severity": "medium",
46:             "fix_template": "converter_api"
47:         },
48:         "nullptr_modifier_in_tests": {
49:             "regex": r"modifier_test_base\.h.*modifier_.*!=.*nullptr",
50:             "category": "static_modifier",
51:             "severity": "high",
52:             "fix_template": "static_modifier"
53:         },
54:         "test_build_config_missing_files": {
55:             "regex": r"undefined reference.*static_modifier",
56:             "category": "build_config",
57:             "severity": "high",
58:             "fix_template": "build_config"
59:         }
60:     }
61: 
62: PATTERNS = load_patterns()
63: 
64: 
65: class CAPITestDiagnoser:
66:     """Main class for diagnosing C API test failures."""
67: 
68:     def __init__(self, ace_engine_root: str = None):
69:         """Initialize with ACE Engine root directory."""
70:         if ace_engine_root is None:
71:             # Try to find ACE Engine root
72:             current_dir = os.getcwd()
73:             while current_dir != '/' and not os.path.exists(os.path.join(current_dir, 'CLAUDE.md')):
74:                 current_dir = os.path.dirname(current_dir)
75:             if os.path.exists(os.path.join(current_dir, 'CLAUDE.md')):
76:                 self.root = current_dir
77:             else:
78:                 raise ValueError("Could not find ACE Engine root directory")
79:         else:
80:             self.root = ace_engine_root

1: #!/usr/bin/env python3
2: """
3: Fix test build configuration issues.
4: Adds missing static modifier files to test BUILD.gn.
5: """
6: 
7: import os
8: import re
9: import sys
10: from pathlib import Path
11: from typing import List, Optional
12: 
13: class BuildConfigFixer:
14:     """Fix test build configuration issues."""
15: 
16:     def __init__(self, ace_engine_root: str):
17:         self.root = ace_engine_root
18:         self.build_gn_path = os.path.join(self.root, "test/unittest/capi/BUILD.gn")
19: 
20:     def find_missing_static_modifiers(self) -> List[str]:
21:         """Find static modifier files that are not in BUILD.gn."""
22:         if not os.path.exists(self.build_gn_path):
23:             print(f"BUILD.gn not found: {self.build_gn_path}")
24:             return []
25: 
26:         # Read BUILD.gn to see what's already included
27:         with open(self.build_gn_path, 'r') as f:
28:             build_content = f.read()
29: 
30:         # Find all static modifier files in the codebase
31:         static_modifier_files = []
32:         bridge_dir = os.path.join(self.root, "frameworks/core/components_ng/pattern")
33: 
34:         for dirpath, _, filenames in os.walk(bridge_dir):
35:             for filename in filenames:
36:                 if filename.endswith("_static_modifier.cpp"):
37:                     rel_path = os.path.relpath(os.path.join(dirpath, filename), self.root)
38:                     static_modifier_files.append(rel_path)
39: 
40:         # Check which ones are missing from BUILD.gn
41:         missing_files = []
42:         for file_path in static_modifier_files:
43:             # Convert to BUILD.gn format
44:             gn_path = f"\"$ace_root/{file_path}\","
45:             if gn_path not in build_content:
46:                 missing_files.append(file_path)
47: 
48:         return missing_files
49: 
50:     def find_static_modifier_for_component(self, component: str) -> Optional[str]:
51:         """Find static modifier file for a specific component."""
52:         component_lower = component.lower()
53:         possible_paths = [
54:             f"frameworks/core/components_ng/pattern/{component_lower}/bridge/{component_lower}_static_modifier.cpp",
55:             f"frameworks/core/components_ng/pattern/{component}/bridge/{component}_static_modifier.cpp",
56:         ]
57: 
58:         for rel_path in possible_paths:
59:             full_path = os.path.join(self.root, rel_path)
60:             if os.path.exists(full_path):
61:                 return rel_path
62: 
63:         # Search for the file
64:         search_pattern = f"*{component_lower}*static_modifier.cpp"
65:         for dirpath, _, filenames in os.walk(os.path.join(self.root, "frameworks/core/components_ng/pattern")):
66:             for filename in filenames:
67:                 if component_lower in filename.lower() and "static_modifier.cpp" in filename:
68:                     rel_path = os.path.relpath(os.path.join(dirpath, filename), self.root)
69:                     return rel_path
70: 
71:         return None
72: 
73:     def add_file_to_build_gn(self, file_path: str) -> bool:
74:         """Add a file to BUILD.gn sources."""
75:         if not os.path.exists(self.build_gn_path):
76:             print(f"BUILD.gn not found: {self.build_gn_path}")
77:             return False
78: 
79:         # Convert to BUILD.gn format
80:         gn_path = f"\"$ace_root/{file_path}\","

1: #!/usr/bin/env python3
2: """
3: Fix Converter API misuse in C API tests.
4: Replaces Converter::GetOptPtr(&var) with Converter::GetOpt(var).
5: """
6: 
7: import os
8: import re
9: import sys
10: from pathlib import Path
11: from typing import List, Tuple
12: 
13: class ConverterAPIFixer:
14:     """Fix Converter API misuse issues."""
15: 
16:     def __init__(self, ace_engine_root: str):
17:         self.root = ace_engine_root
18:         self.pattern = re.compile(r'Converter::GetOptPtr\s*\(\s*&([a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_]*)\s*\)')
19: 
20:     def find_test_files(self) -> List[str]:
21:         """Find C API test files that might have Converter API issues."""
22:         test_files = []
23:         test_dir = os.path.join(self.root, "test/unittest/capi")
24: 
25:         for dirpath, _, filenames in os.walk(test_dir):
26:             for filename in filenames:
27:                 if filename.endswith("_test.cpp") or filename.endswith("_accessor_test.cpp"):
28:                     test_files.append(os.path.join(dirpath, filename))
29: 
30:         return test_files
31: 
32:     def find_converter_api_issues(self, file_path: str) -> List[Tuple[int, str, str]]:
33:         """Find Converter::GetOptPtr(&var) issues in file."""
34:         issues = []
35: 
36:         with open(file_path, 'r') as f:
37:             lines = f.readlines()
38: 
39:         for i, line in enumerate(lines, 1):
40:             matches = self.pattern.findall(line)
41:             for var_name in matches:
42:                 issues.append((i, line.strip(), var_name))
43: 
44:         return issues
45: 
46:     def fix_file(self, file_path: str) -> bool:
47:         """Fix Converter API issues in a file."""
48:         with open(file_path, 'r') as f:
49:             content = f.read()
50: 
51:         # Find all matches
52:         matches = list(self.pattern.finditer(content))
53:         if not matches:
54:             return False
55: 
56:         # Backup original
57:         backup_path = file_path + ".bak"
58:         with open(backup_path, 'w') as f:
59:             f.write(content)
60: 
61:         # Replace in reverse order to preserve positions
62:         for match in reversed(matches):
63:             var_name = match.group(1)
64:             old_expr = match.group(0)
65:             new_expr = f'Converter::GetOpt({var_name})'
66: 
67:             start, end = match.span()
68:             content = content[:start] + new_expr + content[end:]
69: 
70:         # Write fixed content
71:         with open(file_path, 'w') as f:
72:             f.write(content)
73: 
74:         print(f"Fixed {len(matches)} Converter API issue(s) in {file_path}")
75:         print(f"Backup saved to {backup_path}")
76: 
77:         return True
78: 
79:     def update_test_expectations(self, file_path: str) -> bool:
80:         """Update test expectations from nullptr to nullopt."""

1: #!/usr/bin/env python3
2: """
3: Fix static modifier missing issues in C API tests.
4: Adds ARKUI_CAPI_UNITTEST support to modifier files and updates build configuration.
5: """
6: 
7: import os
8: import re
9: import sys
10: from pathlib import Path
11: from typing import Optional
12: 
13: class StaticModifierFixer:
14:     """Fix static modifier issues in C API tests."""
15: 
16:     def __init__(self, ace_engine_root: str):
17:         self.root = ace_engine_root
18:         self.modifier_template = """#ifdef ARKUI_CAPI_UNITTEST
19: const GENERATED_ArkUI{component}Modifier* Get{component}StaticModifier();
20: #endif
21: 
22: const GENERATED_ArkUI{component}Modifier* Get{component}Modifier()
23: {{
24:     static const GENERATED_ArkUI{component}Modifier* cachedModifier = nullptr;
25: 
26:     if (cachedModifier == nullptr) {{
27: #ifdef ARKUI_CAPI_UNITTEST
28:         cachedModifier = GeneratedModifier::Get{component}StaticModifier();
29: #else
30:         auto* module = DynamicModuleHelper::GetInstance().GetDynamicModule("{component}");
31:         CHECK_NULL_RETURN(module, nullptr);
32:         cachedModifier = reinterpret_cast<const GENERATED_ArkUI{component}Modifier*>(module->GetStaticModifier());
33: #endif
34:     }}
35: 
36:     return cachedModifier;
37: }}"""
38: 
39:     def find_modifier_file(self, component: str) -> Optional[str]:
40:         """Find modifier.cpp file for given component."""
41:         component_lower = component.lower()
42:         possible_paths = [
43:             f"frameworks/core/interfaces/native/implementation/{component_lower}_modifier.cpp",
44:             f"frameworks/core/interfaces/native/implementation/{component}Modifier.cpp",
45:         ]
46: 
47:         for rel_path in possible_paths:
48:             full_path = os.path.join(self.root, rel_path)
49:             if os.path.exists(full_path):
50:                 return full_path
51: 
52:         # Try to search
53:         for dirpath, _, filenames in os.walk(os.path.join(self.root, "frameworks/core/interfaces/native")):
54:             for filename in filenames:
55:                 if component_lower in filename.lower() and "modifier.cpp" in filename:
56:                     return os.path.join(dirpath, filename)
57: 
58:         return None
59: 
60:     def check_has_arkui_capi_unittest(self, file_path: str) -> bool:
61:         """Check if file already has ARKUI_CAPI_UNITTEST support."""
62:         if not os.path.exists(file_path):
63:             return False
64: 
65:         with open(file_path, 'r') as f:
66:             content = f.read()
67:

1: #!/usr/bin/env python3
2: """
3: C API Test Naming Verifier
4: 
5: This script verifies that all C API unit test names follow the standardized
6: naming convention: methodNameTestScenario
7: 
8: Usage:
9:     python verify_naming.py [options]
10: 
11: Options:
12:     --verify               Verify all test names (default)
13:     --report               Generate detailed compliance report
14:     --directory DIR        Scan specific directory
15:     --file FILE            Check specific file
16:     --exclude-generated    Exclude generated test directories
17:     --help                 Show this help message
18: """
19: 
20: import os
21: import re
22: import sys
23: import argparse
24: from collections import Counter
25: from pathlib import Path
26: from typing import List, Tuple, Dict, Optional
27: 
28: def find_ace_engine_root(start_path: Path) -> Path:
29:     """Find the ace_engine root directory by looking for marker files."""
30:     current = start_path.absolute()
31:     markers = [".gn", "CLAUDE.md", "test/unittest/capi"]
32: 
33:     while current != current.parent:  # Stop at root
34:         # Check if any marker exists
35:         for marker in markers:
36:             marker_path = current / marker
37:             if marker_path.exists():
38:                 return current
39:         current = current.parent
40: 
41:     # Fallback to start_path parent's parent (should be ace_engine)
42:     return start_path.parent.parent.parent.parent
43: 
44: # Directories to scan (relative to ace_engine root)
45: BASE_DIR = find_ace_engine_root(Path(__file__))
46: DEFAULT_DIRS = [
47:     "test/unittest/capi/modifiers",
48:     "test/unittest/capi/accessors",
49:     "test/unittest/capi/utils",
50: ]
51: 
52: # Exclude generated directories
53: EXCLUDE_DIRS = ["generated"]
54: 
55: # Regex to match HWTEST_F macros and extract test class and name
56: HWTEST_F_REGEX = re.compile(r'HWTEST_F\s*\(\s*(\w+)\s*,\s*(\w+)\s*,', re.MULTILINE)
57: 
58: # Regex to match @tc.name tags (should match test name)
59: TC_NAME_REGEX = re.compile(r'@tc\.name:\s*(\w+)', re.MULTILINE)
60: 
61: # Regex to find line numbers for error reporting
62: LINE_SPLIT_REGEX = re.compile(r'\n')
63: 
64: # Regex to find method calls in test bodies
65: ACCESSOR_METHOD_REGEX = re.compile(r'accessor_->([A-Za-z0-9_]+)')
66: MODIFIER_METHOD_REGEX = re.compile(r'modifier_->([A-Za-z0-9_]+)')
67: COMMON_MODIFIER_METHOD_REGEX = re.compile(r'commonModifier_->([A-Za-z0-9_]+)')
68: 
69: def extract_test_body(content: str, start_pos: int) -> str:
70:     """Extract the test body starting from the given position."""
71:     # Find the opening brace after the HWTEST_F macro
72:     brace_pos = content.find('{', start_pos)
73:     if brace_pos == -1:
74:         return ""
75: 
76:     # Count braces to find matching closing brace
77:     brace_count = 1
78:     pos = brace_pos + 1
79: 
80:     while pos < len(content) and brace_count > 0:

--- .claude/skills/compile-analysis/scripts/analyze_compile.sh (truncated) ---
1: #!/bin/bash
2: #
3: # 编译效率分析脚本
4: # 功能：分析单个文件的编译时间、资源开销和头文件依赖
5: #
6: # ⚠️ 重要要求：
7: #   1. 编译命令必须在 out/{product} 目录下执行
8: #   2. 分析结果必须基于实际运行结果，不能推测
9: #   3. 头文件依赖必须通过 parse_ii.py 解析 .ii 文件
10: #
11: # 使用方法:
12: #   ./analyze_compile.sh <源文件路径> [产品名称] [选项]
13: #
14: # 示例:
15: #   ./analyze_compile.sh frameworks/core/event/touch_event.cpp
16: #   ./analyze_compile.sh frameworks/core/event/touch_event.cpp rk3568
17: #   ./analyze_compile.sh frameworks/core/event/touch_event.cpp rk3568 --save-script
18: #
19: # 选项:
20: #   --save-script    保存增强编译脚本到 out/{product}/compile_single_file_{name}.sh
21: #                    可用于后续重复执行独立的性能测试
22: 
23: set -e
24: 
25: # 获取脚本所在目录
26: SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
27: 
28: # 查找 OpenHarmony 根目录
29: find_oh_root() {
30:     local dir="$(pwd)"
31:     while [[ ! -f "$dir/.gn" ]]; do
32:         dir="$(dirname "$dir")"
33:         if [[ "$dir" == "/" ]]; then
34:             echo "错误: 找不到 OpenHarmony 根目录（无 .gn 文件）" >&2
35:             return 1
36:         fi
37:     done
38:     echo "$dir"
39: }
40: 
41: # 查找 ace_engine 目录
42: find_ace_engine_root() {
43:     local oh_root="$1"
44:     if [[ -d "$oh_root/foundation/arkui/ace_engine" ]]; then
45:         echo "$oh_root/foundation/arkui/ace_engine"
46:     else
47:         echo "错误: 找不到 ace_engine 目录" >&2
48:         return 1
49:     fi
50: }
51: 
52: # 检查参数
53: if [[ $# -lt 1 ]]; then
54:     echo "用法: $0 <源文件路径> [产品名称] [选项]"
55:     echo ""
56:     echo "示例:"
57:     echo "  $0 frameworks/core/event/touch_event.cpp"
58:     echo "  $0 frameworks/core/event/touch_event.cpp rk3568"
59:     echo "  $0 frameworks/core/event/touch_event.cpp rk3568 --save-script"
60:     echo ""
61:     echo "选项:"
62:     echo "  --save-script    保存增强编译脚本到 out/{product}/compile_single_file_{name}.sh"
63:     exit 1
64: fi
65: 
66: SOURCE_FILE="$1"
67: PRODUCT_NAME="${2:-rk3568}"
68: SAVE_SCRIPT=""
69: if [[ "$3" == "--save-script" ]] || [[ "$2" == "--save-script" ]]; then
70:     SAVE_SCRIPT="--save-enhanced"
71: fi
72: 
73: # 查找目录
74: OH_ROOT=$(find_oh_root)
75: ACE_ENGINE_ROOT=$(find_ace_engine_root "$OH_ROOT")
76: 
77: echo "========================================"
78: echo "编译效率分析工具"
79: echo "========================================"
80: echo ""

1: #!/usr/bin/env python3

8: 示例:

10:     python3 get_compile_command.py foundation/arkui/ace_engine/frameworks/core/components_ng/base/frame_node.cpp

13: import os

19: def find_ninja_files(out_dir):
20:     """查找所有相关的ninja文件"""
21:     ninja_files = []
22: 
23:     # 查找toolchain.ninja
24:     toolchain_ninja = os.path.join(out_dir, "toolchain.ninja")
25:     if os.path.exists(toolchain_ninja):
26:         ninja_files.append(('toolchain', toolchain_ninja))
27: 
28:     # 查找所有的子ninja文件
29:     obj_dir = os.path.join(out_dir, "obj")
30:     if os.path.exists(obj_dir):
31:         for root, dirs, files in os.walk(obj_dir):
32:             for file in files:
33:                 if file.endswith('.ninja'):
34:                     ninja_files.append(('sub', os.path.join(root, file)))
35: 
36:     return ninja_files
37: 
38: 
39: def parse_cxx_rule(toolchain_ninja):
40:     """从toolchain.ninja解析cxx规则"""
41:     with open(toolchain_ninja, 'r', encoding='utf-8', errors='ignore') as f:
42:         lines = f.readlines()
43: 
44:     cxx_command = None
45:     in_cxx_rule = False
46: 
47:     for line in lines:
48:         if line.strip() == 'rule cxx':
49:             in_cxx_rule = True
50:             continue
51: 
52:         if in_cxx_rule:
53:             if line.strip().startswith('command ='):
54:                 cxx_command = line.split('=', 1)[1].strip()
55:                 break
56:             elif line.strip() and not line.startswith(' '):
57:                 # 遇到新的规则，退出
58:                 break
59: 
60:     return cxx_command
61: 
62: 
63: def parse_variables(ninja_file):
64:     """解析ninja文件中的所有变量"""
65:     variables = {}
66: 
67:     with open(ninja_file, 'r', encoding='utf-8', errors='ignore') as f:
68:         lines = f.readlines()
69: 
70:     for line in lines:
71:         # 解析变量定义 (var = value)
72:         if '=' in line and not line.strip().startswith('#'):
73:             # 跳过build规则
74:             if line.strip().startswith('build '):
75:                 continue
76:             # 跳过rule定义
77:             if line.strip().startswith('rule '):
78:                 continue
79: 
80:             parts = line.split('=', 1)

--- .claude/skills/compile-analysis/scripts/parse_ii.py (truncated) ---
1: #!/usr/bin/env python3
2: 
3: import re
4: import argparse
5: from collections import defaultdict
6: 
7: def parse_ii_file(file_path):
8:     file_include_pattern = re.compile(r'#\s*\d+\s+"([^"]+)"')
9:     target_prefix = "foundation/arkui/"
10:     dependencies = []
11:     stack = []
12: 
13:     with open(file_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as file:
14:         for line in file:
15:             match = file_include_pattern.match(line)
16:             if match:
17:                 new_file = match.group(1)
18:                 if target_prefix in new_file:
19:                     if new_file in stack:
20:                         index = stack.index(new_file)
21:                         dependencies.append(stack[:index + 1])
22:                         stack = stack[:index]
23:                     stack.append(new_file)
24: 
25:     if stack:
26:         dependencies.append(stack)
27: 
28:     return dependencies
29: 
30: def build_tree_structure(dependencies):
31:     tree = {}
32:     for path in dependencies:
33:         current_level = tree
34:         for file in path:
35:             if file not in current_level:
36:                 current_level[file] = {}
37:             current_level = current_level[file]
38:     return tree
39: 
40: def print_tree(tree, prefix='', is_last=True, output_file=None):
41:     items = list(tree.items())
42:     count = len(items)
43:     for i, (file, subtree) in enumerate(items):
44:         connector = '└──' if i == count - 1 else '├──'
45:         new_prefix = prefix + ('    ' if i == count - 1 else '│   ')
46: 
47:         line = prefix + connector + ' ' + file
48:         print(line)
49: 
50:         # 如果指定了输出文件，写入文件
51:         if output_file:
52:             output_file.write(line + '\n')
53: 
54:         # 递归处理子节点
55:         print_tree(subtree, new_prefix, is_last=i == count - 1, output_file=output_file)
56: 
57: def main(file_path, output_path=None):
58:     dependencies = parse_ii_file(file_path)
59:     tree = build_tree_structure(dependencies)
60: 
61:     header = "头文件的依赖关系树："
62:     print(header)
63: 
64:     output_file = None
65:     if output_path:
66:         output_file = open(output_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8')
67:         output_file.write(header + '\n')
68: 
69:     try:
70:         print_tree(tree, output_file=output_file)
71:     finally:
72:         if output_file:
73:             output_file.close()
74:             print(f"\n✓ 依赖树已保存到: {output_path}")
75: 
76: if __name__ == "__main__":
77:     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description='解析 .ii 文件并显示头文件依赖关系树。')
78:     parser.add_argument('file', type=str, help='要解析的 .ii 文件路径')
79:     parser.add_argument('--output', '-o', type=str, help='保存依赖树到指定文件（可选）')
80:     args = parser.parse_args()

Developer documentation requirements:
- Include source anchors for important claims: file path, visible symbol/function/class name when available, and why the anchor matters.
- Include a runbook when operational evidence exists: build, validation, deployment, rollback, and failure-mode notes.
- Include debugging guidance for each major module: symptom, likely source area, and command or file to inspect.
- State certainty boundaries: mark source-confirmed facts separately from inferred behavior or missing evidence.
